【检索到的指南段落】
--- chunk aa2024_41 | CLINICAL STAGING > Stage 0 ---
The change we propose in clinical staging from 2018 is the addition of stage 0. Stage 0 represents part of the AD continuum and is defined as genetically determined AD (which includes ADAD or DSAD) 46 , 160 in an individual who is biomarker negative and clinically asymptomatic (Table 6). The rationale is that these individuals have the disease from birth, prior to onset of brain pathologic change or symptoms. A person with DSAD or ADAD would move from stage 0 into stage 1 when a diagnostic Core 1 biomarker(s) became positive. The idea of stage 0 as genetically determined disease that has not yet manifested clinically or with biomarkers is conceptually consistent with recent staging proposals for Huntington's and NSD. 57 , 58 , 59

--- chunk aa2024_44 | INTEGRATED BIOLOGICAL AND CLINICAL STAGING ---
As in the 2018 NIA‐AA framework, clinical staging and biological disease staging in our revised criteria are regarded as quasi‐independent variables. The symptomatic consequence of biological AD is modified by interindividual differences in copathologies, resistance, and reserve (i.e., education and other social determinants of health). 166 , 167 , 168 Consequently, the degree of cognitive/functional impairment does not follow in lockstep with biological AD severity (i.e., a range of possible relationships between biological AD stage and clinical stage will be found across the population; Figure 1). While clinical staging and biological staging must be performed independently, these two types of staging information can be integrated while still preserving independence of content.

As shown in Table 7, we propose an integrated biological and clinical staging scheme in which clinical stages are denoted in the columns using the numeric six‐stage scheme plus stage 0. Biological stages are denoted in the rows. Integrated stages appear in the cells. This format is intended to convey the concept that biological AD stage and clinical severity are related, but do not move in lockstep in all individuals.

[TABLE]
Integrated biological and clinical staging.
 | Stage 0 | Clinical Stage 1 | Clinical Stage 2 | Clinical Stage 3 | Clinical Stages 4–6
Initial biological stage (A) | X | 1A | 2A | 3A | 4‐6A
Early biological stage (B) | X | 1B | 2B | 3B | 4‐6B
Intermediate biological stage (C) | X | 1C | 2C | 3C | 4‐6C
Advanced biological stage (D) | X | 1D | 2D | 3D | 4‐6D

--- chunk aa2024_17 | DIAGNOSIS > Anchoring Core 1 biomarkers for AD diagnosis to reference standards ---
Currently, no BBMs have received regulatory approval for any intended use, although this is expected to change soon. Diagnostic accuracy varies substantially among various plasma p‐tau and Aβ42/40 assays. 69 , 70 , 71 Accuracy estimates with respect to an amyloid PET or CSF reference standard using a single preselected cutpoint or area under the receiver operating curve (i.e., accuracy over all cutpoints) range from 0.6 (60%) to > 0.9 (90%). 69 , 72 , 73 , 74 , 75 Thus, some plasma assays, particularly some p‐tau217 assays alone or in combination, have accuracy that is equivalent to that of approved CSF assays. 69 , 74 , 76 , 77 Accuracy must be defined in the intended‐use population. Thus, our definition of Core 1 plasma tests that suffice as standalone diagnostic tests for AD is those with a minimum accuracy of 90% to detect abnormal amyloid PET in the intended‐use population, or, more simply, plasma tests whose diagnostic performance is equivalent to that of approved CSF assays (Box 2). Our position is that BBM that achieve this diagnostic performance benchmark should be considered on equal footing with established PET and CSF biomarkers for diagnosis of AD.

To date, clinical symptoms have not been used as the reference standard for regulatory approval of AD biomarkers. Our position is that AD is defined by its biology and therefore a biomarker that can accurately detect ADNPC or a validated surrogate is sufficient to establish the diagnosis of the disease.

--- chunk aa2024_14 | DIAGNOSIS > Rationale for diagnosis of AD by Core 1 biomarkers ---
To address diagnosing AD by biomarkers in cognitively unimpaired individuals, we examined the Arizona Study of Aging and Neurodegenerative Disorders and Brain and Body Donation Program 63 database and found 123 individuals who were cognitively unimpaired at death and had moderate/frequent CERAD neuritic plaque scores. One hundred seven (87%) of these were also Braak III to VI and thus met criteria for intermediate/high ADNPC. The December 2023 NACC data freeze mentioned above contained 252 asymptomatic (CDR 0) individuals with moderate/frequent neuritic plaques and who also had been assigned a Braak NFT stage; 186/252 (74%) were Braak III to VI, while 226/252 (91%) were Braak II to VI. Thus, we can conclude that while most (74%−87%) asymptomatic individuals with an abnormal amyloid PET scan (i.e., moderate/frequent neuritic plaques) will meet criteria for intermediate/high ADNPC at that time, a proportion will not. But nearly all (91%) will either meet criteria at that time or show evidence of progression of NFT pathology beyond the entorhinal cortex (i.e., Braak II–VI).b:T1af3,【evidence 摘要】

【铁律】
1. 只允许使用随附 evidence JSON 与图像中的信息;任何结论必须引用具体数值(例如 hippocampus_l=3.12 ml、qc_score=0.83)。
2. 禁止编造数值、文献、检查结果;禁止假设存在未提供的检查——本系统没有 MMSE/CDR 等认知量表、没有 PET/脑脊液生物标志物、没有病史与主诉。
3. 你不知道、也绝不可声称知道该例的真实诊断标签;evidence 中不包含标签。
4. status 为 failed/pending/disabled 的工具,其结果缺失必须如实说明,不得假装存在。其中 pending 表示"本批次未启用该通道"(结果尚未计算),不等于"本应存在而缺失的证据";pending 本身不构成下调置信度或弃权的理由。
5. 输出正文用中文(专业术语可保留英文);输出必须是合法 JSON,字段名用英文,不要输出 JSON 以外的任何文字。
6. 结论必须体现"辅助评估、不能替代临床诊断"的定位。

【决策政策】本系统的职责是在现有证据下给出最可能的阶段判断。模型概率分歧大是常态而非异常,不构成本身的弃权理由。仅当出现以下情况之一才允许输出 Uncertain:(a) 关键证据通道 failed/qc 不合格;(b) 各类证据指向不可调和的矛盾;(c) 图像或分割质量差到不可解释。其余情况必须在 CN/MCI/AD 中选择证据最支持的一类,残余疑虑通过 confidence("high|medium|low")表达,不得用 Uncertain 回避判断。

【输入说明】evidence JSON 字段:status(各工具状态)、images(图像路径)、synthseg(体积 ml、icv_ml、qc_score)、freesurfer(etiv、aseg 体积、皮层厚度,可能为 null)、models(四个独立训练的 3D CNN 分类器对 CN/MCI/AD 的概率)、derived(由 Calculator 计算的派生指标,你不许自己重新计算)、warnings(质控警告)。

【测量口径警示】SynthSeg 的体积口径系统性偏大于 FreeSurfer 与多数文献值(其 ICV 含全部 CSF,约 1900-2100 ml;其海马体积相应偏大)。**禁止把 synthseg 绝对体积与文献中的典型海马体积(约 3-3.5 ml/侧)直接对照**——那会系统性误判为"无萎缩"。更可靠的用法:(a) 头大小归一化的 ratio 指标(hippo_ratio、ventricle_ratio);(b) 左右不对称度;(c) 多个结构的组合模式(海马+下侧脑室+脑室扩大同时存在更有提示意义);(d) 图像上的形态学所见(脑室扩大、颞角增宽、脑沟增宽)。没有可靠外部常模时,承认绝对分级的不确定性,但仍须按【决策政策】给出最可能类别。11:T6be,病例 C101 | 70 岁 M | eTIV 1523.8 ml | z 口径:映射正常模型(r≥0.9)优先,否则队列内留一折稳健 z | 证据组 MRI_SINGLE_SCAN

【检索到的指南段落】
--- chunk aa2024_41 | CLINICAL STAGING > Stage 0 ---
The change we propose in clinical staging from 2018 is the addition of stage 0. Stage 0 represents part of the AD continuum and is defined as genetically determined AD (which includes ADAD or DSAD) 46 , 160 in an individual who is biomarker negative and clinically asymptomatic (Table 6). The rationale is that these individuals have the disease from birth, prior to onset of brain pathologic change or symptoms. A person with DSAD or ADAD would move from stage 0 into stage 1 when a diagnostic Core 1 biomarker(s) became positive. The idea of stage 0 as genetically determined disease that has not yet manifested clinically or with biomarkers is conceptually consistent with recent staging proposals for Huntington's and NSD. 57 , 58 , 59

--- chunk aa2024_44 | INTEGRATED BIOLOGICAL AND CLINICAL STAGING ---
As in the 2018 NIA‐AA framework, clinical staging and biological disease staging in our revised criteria are regarded as quasi‐independent variables. The symptomatic consequence of biological AD is modified by interindividual differences in copathologies, resistance, and reserve (i.e., education and other social determinants of health). 166 , 167 , 168 Consequently, the degree of cognitive/functional impairment does not follow in lockstep with biological AD severity (i.e., a range of possible relationships between biological AD stage and clinical stage will be found across the population; Figure 1). While clinical staging and biological staging must be performed independently, these two types of staging information can be integrated while still preserving independence of content.

As shown in Table 7, we propose an integrated biological and clinical staging scheme in which clinical stages are denoted in the columns using the numeric six‐stage scheme plus stage 0. Biological stages are denoted in the rows. Integrated stages appear in the cells. This format is intended to convey the concept that biological AD stage and clinical severity are related, but do not move in lockstep in all individuals.

[TABLE]
Integrated biological and clinical staging.
 | Stage 0 | Clinical Stage 1 | Clinical Stage 2 | Clinical Stage 3 | Clinical Stages 4–6
Initial biological stage (A) | X | 1A | 2A | 3A | 4‐6A
Early biological stage (B) | X | 1B | 2B | 3B | 4‐6B
Intermediate biological stage (C) | X | 1C | 2C | 3C | 4‐6C
Advanced biological stage (D) | X | 1D | 2D | 3D | 4‐6D

--- chunk aa2024_17 | DIAGNOSIS > Anchoring Core 1 biomarkers for AD diagnosis to reference standards ---
Currently, no BBMs have received regulatory approval for any intended use, although this is expected to change soon. Diagnostic accuracy varies substantially among various plasma p‐tau and Aβ42/40 assays. 69 , 70 , 71 Accuracy estimates with respect to an amyloid PET or CSF reference standard using a single preselected cutpoint or area under the receiver operating curve (i.e., accuracy over all cutpoints) range from 0.6 (60%) to > 0.9 (90%). 69 , 72 , 73 , 74 , 75 Thus, some plasma assays, particularly some p‐tau217 assays alone or in combination, have accuracy that is equivalent to that of approved CSF assays. 69 , 74 , 76 , 77 Accuracy must be defined in the intended‐use population. Thus, our definition of Core 1 plasma tests that suffice as standalone diagnostic tests for AD is those with a minimum accuracy of 90% to detect abnormal amyloid PET in the intended‐use population, or, more simply, plasma tests whose diagnostic performance is equivalent to that of approved CSF assays (Box 2). Our position is that BBM that achieve this diagnostic performance benchmark should be considered on equal footing with established PET and CSF biomarkers for diagnosis of AD.

To date, clinical symptoms have not been used as the reference standard for regulatory approval of AD biomarkers. Our position is that AD is defined by its biology and therefore a biomarker that can accurately detect ADNPC or a validated surrogate is sufficient to establish the diagnosis of the disease.

--- chunk aa2024_14 | DIAGNOSIS > Rationale for diagnosis of AD by Core 1 biomarkers ---
To address diagnosing AD by biomarkers in cognitively unimpaired individuals, we examined the Arizona Study of Aging and Neurodegenerative Disorders and Brain and Body Donation Program 63 database and found 123 individuals who were cognitively unimpaired at death and had moderate/frequent CERAD neuritic plaque scores. One hundred seven (87%) of these were also Braak III to VI and thus met criteria for intermediate/high ADNPC. The December 2023 NACC data freeze mentioned above contained 252 asymptomatic (CDR 0) individuals with moderate/frequent neuritic plaques and who also had been assigned a Braak NFT stage; 186/252 (74%) were Braak III to VI, while 226/252 (91%) were Braak II to VI. Thus, we can conclude that while most (74%−87%) asymptomatic individuals with an abnormal amyloid PET scan (i.e., moderate/frequent neuritic plaques) will meet criteria for intermediate/high ADNPC at that time, a proportion will not. But nearly all (91%) will either meet criteria at that time or show evidence of progression of NFT pathology beyond the entorhinal cortex (i.e., Braak II–VI).17:T1a83,【evidence 摘要】

{"citations": [{"source": "AA2024", "chunk_id": "aa2024_17", "quote": "AD is defined by its biology and therefore a biomarker that can accurately detect ADNPC or a validated surrogate is sufficient to establish the diagnosis of the disease."}, {"source": "AA2024", "chunk_id": "aa2024_44", "quote": "clinical staging and biological staging must be performed independently"}], "framework_mapping": "本例仅有结构MRI（SynthSeg体积、图像形态）与影像模型，属于N类（神经变性）证据，且SynthSeg显示海马/侧脑室比值未提示显著萎缩，与初判CN一致。但AA2024框架要求以Core 1生物标志物（Aβ/PET/CSF）定义AD生物分期，本系统没有A/T生物标志物，也没有认知量表确定临床分期，因此无法完成AT(N)定性，只能提供结构层面的辅助评估。", "note": "AA2024以生物标志物（A/T）定义AD：A（Aβ）与T（tau）为Core 1，N为神经变性，本例仅有结构MRI（可视为N类信息）+影像模型，未涉及A/T，故不能依据AA2024进行AD生物分期定性；结构MRI未见明确萎缩支持初判CN，但不能排除临床前AD或A/T阳性但尚未出现结构改变的可能。本系统为课程演示，非临床诊断工具。"}

{"alternative_explanations": ["正常老龄化萎缩：本患者 82 岁，SynthSeg 显示脑沟轻度增宽、皮层体积如 ctx_lh_superiorfrontal=24.161 ml 与 ctx_rh_superiorfrontal=24.4518 ml 尚可，但存在与年龄相符的体积下降；海马双侧 3.5456/3.4592 ml 在 SynthSeg 口径下并不提示病理性萎缩，故正常老化是最合理的背景性解释，不构成对 CN 的否定。", "血管性损害：本系统仅有 T1 与体积指标，无 FLAIR/弥散序列，无法直接观察 WMH 或梗死；SynthSeg 未见局灶性体积缺失，皮层下结构（丘脑 9.32/9.27 ml、尾状核 3.72/3.62 ml、壳核 7.96/7.53 ml）相对对称且无明显局部萎缩，故无证据支持血管性痴呆，但需申明可见性限制。", "抑郁等假性痴呆：本系统没有认知量表、病史或主诉，无法评估抑郁等可逆性认知障碍；结构 MRI 本身无特异性改变，既不能支持也不能排除，因证据缺失，不应作为优先替代解释。", "正常压力脑积水（NPH）：不支持。ventricle_ratio=0.0118，侧脑室左右 12.0585/11.6632 ml 未见显著扩大；下侧脑室 1.5783/1.9964 ml 无明显增宽；图像未见典型 NPH 的脑室扩张与脑沟不成比例变小，故 NPH 解释缺乏证据。", "其他神经变性病（FTD/DLB 等）：不支持。额叶皮层体积（ctx_lh_superiorfrontal=24.161 ml、ctx_rh_superiorfrontal=24.4518 ml，rostralmiddlefrontal 左右 18.75/17.46 ml）未见明显局灶性萎缩；无不对称性病理模式；无脑干或基底节特征性改变提示 PSP/DLB，故无证据支持。", "技术因素：SynthSeg qc_score=0.8539，qc_min=0.7699，warnings 为空，分割状态 ok；FreeSurfer 为 disabled 而非 failed，其缺失是通道未启用而非质控失败；图像（axial/coronal/sagittal/mtl）可解释，未见脑部结构错位或严重分割伪影，故技术因素并非当前结论的替代解释。"], "downgrade_suggested": false, "reasoning": "Primary 初判 CN，与其证据一致：SynthSeg qc=0.8539 合格，hippo_total=7.0048 ml、hippo_ratio_synthseg=0.003485，侧脑室比例 ventricle_ratio=0.0118 均不支持病理性萎缩；四个 CNN 模型一致倾向 CN（CN=0.8539, MCI=0.1456, AD=0.0004）。在系统测量口径下，海马绝对体积不能直接与文献阈值对照，但头校正比值、不对称度（0.02467）与形态均无 AD 模式。各类替代解释中，正常老化是最合理背景；血管性损害、NPH、FTD/DLB 因缺乏对应证据而不适用；抑郁等假性痴呆缺乏评估工具；技术因素无质控失败证据。决策政策要求在上述条件下给出最可能类别而非 Uncertain，故维持 CN，且无需下调到更不严重方向（因为 CN 本身即为最不严重类别），downgrade_suggested=false。本结论仍属辅助评估，不能替代临床诊断。"}1a:T37ff,【evidence】

【铁律】
1. 只允许使用随附 evidence JSON 与图像中的信息;任何结论必须引用具体数值(例如 hippocampus_l=3.12 ml、qc_score=0.83)。
2. 禁止编造数值、文献、检查结果;禁止假设存在未提供的检查——本系统没有 MMSE/CDR 等认知量表、没有 PET/脑脊液生物标志物、没有病史与主诉。
3. 你不知道、也绝不可声称知道该例的真实诊断标签;evidence 中不包含标签。
4. status 为 failed/pending/disabled 的工具,其结果缺失必须如实说明,不得假装存在。其中 pending 表示"本批次未启用该通道"(结果尚未计算),不等于"本应存在而缺失的证据";pending 本身不构成下调置信度或弃权的理由。
5. 输出正文用中文(专业术语可保留英文);输出必须是合法 JSON,字段名用英文,不要输出 JSON 以外的任何文字。
6. 结论必须体现"辅助评估、不能替代临床诊断"的定位。

【决策政策】本系统的职责是在现有证据下给出最可能的阶段判断。模型概率分歧大是常态而非异常,不构成本身的弃权理由。仅当出现以下情况之一才允许输出 Uncertain:(a) 关键证据通道 failed/qc 不合格;(b) 各类证据指向不可调和的矛盾;(c) 图像或分割质量差到不可解释。其余情况必须在 CN/MCI/AD 中选择证据最支持的一类,残余疑虑通过 confidence("high|medium|low")表达,不得用 Uncertain 回避判断。

【输入说明】evidence JSON 字段:status(各工具状态)、images(图像路径)、synthseg(体积 ml、icv_ml、qc_score)、freesurfer(etiv、aseg 体积、皮层厚度,可能为 null)、models(四个独立训练的 3D CNN 分类器对 CN/MCI/AD 的概率)、derived(由 Calculator 计算的派生指标,你不许自己重新计算)、warnings(质控警告)。

【测量口径警示】SynthSeg 的体积口径系统性偏大于 FreeSurfer 与多数文献值(其 ICV 含全部 CSF,约 1900-2100 ml;其海马体积相应偏大)。**禁止把 synthseg 绝对体积与文献中的典型海马体积(约 3-3.5 ml/侧)直接对照**——那会系统性误判为"无萎缩"。更可靠的用法:(a) 头大小归一化的 ratio 指标(hippo_ratio、ventricle_ratio);(b) 左右不对称度;(c) 多个结构的组合模式(海马+下侧脑室+脑室扩大同时存在更有提示意义);(d) 图像上的形态学所见(脑室扩大、颞角增宽、脑沟增宽)。没有可靠外部常模时,承认绝对分级的不确定性,但仍须按【决策政策】给出最可能类别。1d:T6c9,病例 C137 | 82 岁 M | eTIV 1477.5 ml | z 口径:映射正常模型(r≥0.9)优先,否则队列内留一折稳健 z | 证据组 MRI_SINGLE_SCAN
